### Job description

To make electronics, you need chips, wafers, transistors, reticles, and... To make these, you must see, test and manufacture them at scale—faster and better than ever before. That's where KLA comes in. Whether you're early in your career or an experienced professional, you'll solve complex challenges, work alongside brilliant minds and help shape the future of technology. Group/Division KLA's Global Supply Chain (GSC) oversees sourcing, supplier contracting and management, materials management, direct and indirect procurement, and logistics and trade compliance. GSC leads activities to establish and manage an innovative, reliable, efficient, effective and compliant global supply chain and logistics structure that strengthens KLA's competitive advantage. What You'll Do In this role, you will play a key part in advancing business priorities by delivering high-impact work across your area of expertise. Job Description/Preferred Qualifications: We are seeking an early-career import/export analyst to join the Trade Operations team within KLA's Freight and Trade Operations organization. This role is designed for new & recent graduates who want to build deep expertise in data analytics , with a strong emphasis on statistical analysis, data quality measurement, and repeatable analytical workflows applied to global trade data. You will work alongside experienced trade operations professionals and data partners, gaining hands-on experience turning shipment, customs filing, and broker data into reliable, actionable insight. No prior trade or customs experience is required. You will learn the business while contributing technical skills, under guidance, in a team that prioritizes technical excellence, mentorship, and continuous learning. Responsibilities: Collect, clean, join, and analyze data from operational systems, customs broker files, and business documents to build trusted analytical datasets Develop repeatable analyses and automated checks (SQL, Python, or R) that identify missing data, inconsistencies, anomalies, and recurring issues in trade data Apply statistical methods to understand operational performance, trends, and the drivers of data quality problems Build visualizations and dashboards, and communicate findings and recommendations clearly to business stakeholders Translate business data requirements into measurable data quality rules and validation logic, and track improvements with business data owners Support data dictionaries, field mappings, source-to-target mappings, and data lineage documentation for customs and shipment data Support data validation and testing for system and process transformation initiatives Document methods, assumptions, and limitations so analyses are reproducible and reviewable; contribute to forecasting or predictive models where a defined business need and suitable data exist Qualifications: Bachelor's or master's degree in statistics, data science, mathematics, computer science, engineering, or a related quantitative field (0-1 years of work experience); internships, research, and academic projects count as relevant experience Strong foundation in statistics and mathematics (regardless of degree) Experience with Python or R and SQL for data preparation, analysis, and modeling (academic, research, internship, or portfolio work accepted) Experience cleaning and combining multi-source data and presenting results in clear visualizations Strong problem-solving skills, self-motivated, desire for continuous learning and improvement Efficiently and effectively communicate complex topics with technical and non-technical audiences Preferred Qualifications: Interest in learning supply chain, logistics, or global trade processes Experience with a BI tool (e.g., Power BI or Tableau) Portfolio or research project demonstrating reproducible analysis (e.g., version-controlled code) Exposure to data quality, data governance, or ETL concepts Minimum Qualifications Master's Level Degree and 0 years related work experience; Bachelor's Level Degree and related work experience of 2 years About KLA We provide advanced inspection tools, metrology systems, process solutions, and computational analytics that make electronics possible, tackling complex challenges. From electron and photon optics to machine learning and data analytics, we seek perfection at the most fundamental level of matter in the universe. If you want to make electronics that push industries forward and make the world a better place, join us. Total Rewards Base Pay Range: $58,200.00 - $98,900.00 Annually&#xa;Primary Location: USA-AZ-Phoenix-KLA KLA’s total rewards package for employees may also include participation in performance incentive programs and eligibility for additional benefits including but not limited to: medical, dental, vision, life, and other voluntary benefits, 401(K) including company matching, employee stock purchase program (ESPP), student debt assistance, tuition reimbursement program, development and career growth opportunities and programs, financial planning benefits, wellness benefits including an employee assistance program (EAP), paid time off and paid company holidays, and family care and bonding leave. Interns are eligible for some of the benefits listed. Our pay ranges are determined by role, level, and location. The range displayed reflects the pay for this position in the primary location identified in this posting. Actual pay depends on several factors, including state minimum pay wage rates, location, job-related skills, experience, and relevant education level or training. We are committed to complying with all applicable federal and state minimum wage requirements where applicable. If applicable, your recruiter can share more about the specific pay range for your preferred location during the hiring process. Use of AI Statement At KLA, our interviews seek to understand your individual skills, problem-solving approach and authentic thinking. - Type: internship

- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

Our vision is to transform how the world uses information to enrich life for all . Micron Technology is a world leader in innovating memory and storage solutions that accelerate the transformation of information into intelligence, inspiring the world to learn, communicate and advance faster than ever. The AMAIS team (Advanced Modeling & AI Solutions) builds advanced modeling and AI capabilities to accelerate technology development and deliver impactful solutions—moving from predictive modeling toward agentic solution deployments across complex engineering workflows Role Summary As an intern on the Advanced Modeling & AI Solutions (AMAIS) team, you will help support development and prototyping of advanced physics-based and machine learning models, and contribute to agentic AI systems that integrate with modeling tools and engineering workflows. You will also help design and implement user-facing UI experiences (dashboards, apps, or lightweight front ends) that make insights and agent outputs actionable for engineers. This is a hands-on role where you will collaborate with multi-functional engineering partners, contribute production-quality prototypes, and communicate results through clear visualizations and storytelling. Responsibilities Architect and prototype Agentic AI and LLM workflows that use tools, planning, actions, observations, and feedback loops to automate multi-step engineering tasks. Develop RAG-based assistants, machine learning models, physics-based models, and other AI-Enabled solutions that improve engineering analysis, prediction, diagnostics, and decision-making. Build intuitive web applications, dashboards, visualizations, and APIs that translate model and Agent outputs into clear insights, confidence indicators, and recommended actions. Develop repeatable workflows for preparing structured and unstructured data and evaluating model and Agentic AI performance using appropriate metrics, validation methods, and structured evaluations. Partner with engineers and data owners to scope problems, validate assumptions, document solutions, and communicate model performance, limitations, and business or engineering impact. Minimum Qualifications Currently pursuing a BS, MS, or PhD or equivalent experience in Computer Science, Data Science, Electrical Engineering, Computer Engineering, Applied Mathematics, Physics, or a related technical field. Demonstrated programming experience with Python for data preparation, modeling, experimentation, or application development. Coursework, academic research, or project experience applying machine learning fundamentals and frameworks such as PyTorch or TensorFlow. Coursework, research, or project experience with LLMs, Agentic AI, Agent workflows, or RAG, including an understanding of planning, execution, tools, and observation concepts. Demonstrated experience developing user interfaces with modern web technologies such as React and working with APIs or services to display tables, charts, visualizations, or interactive results. Preferred Qualifications Experience with Agentic AI orchestration frameworks such as LangGraph, LangChain, AutoGen, or similar technologies. Experience Using AI for code generation or development workflows, including tools such as Claude Code, or experience developing low-code or no-code solutions. Experience implementing structured evaluation approaches for Agent or LLM quality, including evaluation harnesses, prompt regression testing, or reliability assessments. Experience with CI/CD, MLOps, machine learning pipelines built for real-world applications, or deployment practices. Experience with semiconductor or manufacturing datasets, high-dimensional sensor/process data, numerical methods, physics-informed modeling, multiphysics modeling, multiscale modeling, or machine learning based on simulations. As a world leader in the semiconductor industry, Micron is dedicated to your personal wellbeing and professional growth. Micron benefits are designed to help you stay well, provide peace of mind and help you prepare for the future. We offer a choice of medical, dental and vision plans in all locations enabling team members to select the plans that best meet their family healthcare needs and budget. Micron also provides benefit programs that help protect your income if you are unable to work due to illness or injury, and paid family leave. Additionally, Micron benefits include a robust paid time-off program and paid holidays. For additional information regarding the Benefit programs available, please see the Benefits Guide posted on micron.com/careers/benefits . Micron is proud to be an equal opportunity workplace and is an affirmative action employer. All qualified applicants will receive consideration for employment without regard to race, color, religion, sex, sexual orientation, age, national origin, citizenship status, disability, protected veteran status, gender identity or any other factor protected by applicable federal, state, or local laws. ### Job description

About Citi: Citi, the leading global bank, has approximately 200 million customer accounts and does business in more than 160 countries and jurisdictions. The successful candidate will develop and own quantitative models used to assess battery dispatch, revenues and economics. The candidate will use models to analyze battery storage market opportunities and how they are evolving across global power markets. The rapid deployment of battery storage is changing the way power markets operate. Understanding the value of storage requires increasingly sophisticated modeling of asset operation, electricity prices, market participation and interactions between growing storage capacity and the wider power system. BNEF is a strategic research provider covering global commodity markets and the disruptive technologies driving the transition to a low-carbon economy. Our expert coverage assesses pathways for the power, transport, industry, buildings and agriculture sectors to adapt to the energy transition. We help commodity trading, corporate strategy, finance and policy professionals navigate change and generate opportunities. OUR TEAM: You will be based in our London offices, working as part of a global research team at BloombergNEF (BNEF) – the world's leading independent provider of analysis, tools, data and research to decision makers leading change in the energy system. Our data-driven research helps clients stay on top of developments across the energy sector, supported by the unique depth and breadth of the Bloomberg Terminal and bnef.com platforms. The Solar & Energy Storage team covers the technologies, markets and economics shaping the sector globally. We forecast storage deployment and battery costs, track projects and market activity, assess policy and regulatory developments, and analyze the investment case for storage across different markets and applications. This includes quantitative modelling to understand battery dispatch and revenues, the impact of storage penetration on power markets, and the value of storage across different durations, technologies and market designs. The team translates these dynamics into transparent, data-driven insights for our clients. BNEF TEAM ETHOS: To succeed at BNEF we need people who can work in an independent manner and show initiative to develop their own viewpoints, yet be collaborative with colleagues. We don't rely on the status quo, we look for innovative yet pragmatic thinking that turns big ideas into real insights and impact. Working at BNEF sometimes feels chaotic; we need teams that are both dynamic and structured to generate the unique perspectives our clients really value. Our teams are diverse, creative, focused, and fun! THE ROLE: You will join for an initial six-month contract to lead the development of our in-house energy storage revenue models. You will build, validate and improve tools that optimize the dispatch of standalone and co-located batteries across multiple power markets and revenue streams. As a member of the solar and energy storage team, you will own the development and testing of this model and help your colleagues to blend their subject matter knowledge into the model wherever possible. You will also apply BNEF's models to assess evolving business opportunities in key storage markets. This includes conducting independent research, generating insightful findings, and producing written reports of publishable quality. You will collaborate with a high degree of professionalism with colleagues and clients from different cultures and countries, in person and remotely. You'll also develop an internal and external network of industry contacts in order to gather insights, identify trends and collect information. You will be working with colleagues and clients based around the world. The ideal candidate will be comfortable moving between mathematical formulation, code, data and economic interpretation. You should enjoy building models from first principles, testing whether they accurately represent real-world behavior and continuously improving their methodology as markets and technologies evolve. Just as importantly, you should be able to explain what a model is doing, why its results change and where its limitations lie. BNEF's models ultimately need to produce transparent and defensible insights that can be understood and used by analysts and clients. WE WILL TRUST YOU TO: Design, develop and maintain revenue and dispatch optimization models for battery energy storage – covering wholesale power, ancillary services and capacity markets –building on and enhancing existing BNEF models to scale approach across markets globally, including for co-located storage. Collaborate with multiple analyst teams to discuss and enhance storage modeling approaches used across BNEF, developing, understanding and communicating methodology clearly. Develop approaches for modeling the interaction between increasing storage penetration, electricity-price formation, price spreads and storage revenue capture. Build calibration and backtesting frameworks to compare modeled dispatch and revenues against historical market outcomes and observed battery behavior. Work with large, high-frequency electricity-market datasets, including wholesale prices, ancillary-service prices, generation, load and battery dispatch. Write production-quality Python code that is modular, tested, documented and reproducible with clear documentation of model methodologies, assumptions, limitations and changes. Translate quantitative outputs into economically meaningful conclusions and communicate model results to BNEF analysts and clients, including through published reports written in English. Support regular team publications, including BNEF's Energy Storage Market Outlook and market analysis. Communicate your research to clients via email, telephone and in-person meetings. YOU'LL NEED TO HAVE: A Bachelor's or Master's degree in engineering, economics, computer science, physics or another highly quantitative discipline, or equivalent professional experience. Intellectual curiosity and a demonstrated interest in and enthusiasm for the power or energy storage sector and the role it plays in the transition Knowledge of wholesale electricity markets, including day-ahead, intraday or real-time, balancing and ancillary-service markets. Advanced Python programming skills to deliver robust, intuitive and reliable codebase that others can understand and maintain. Experience formulating and implementing optimization models or other quantitative dispatch models in Python. Experience working with large and complex datasets using Python, Excel and SQL. ### Job description

2027 Summer Intern - AI for Drug Discovery Department Summary The Foundation Models team is seeking exceptional graduate interns with strong research experience in machine learning (ML) and a passion for applying advanced algorithms to scientific discovery. Our team develops state-of-the-art generative models, autonomous scientific agents, and foundational ML methods to accelerate drug design. Autonomous agents increasingly run long scientific workflows in which they must decide, round after round, whether to continue a promising strategy, explore a new one, stop an unproductive experiment, or revisit an earlier hypothesis. Yet we know little about whether these agents learn from experimental evidence the way a good scientist would. This internship will study that question rigorously, using real multi-round drug discovery campaigns. The intern will test whether agents make decisions for the right scientific reasons: whether they learn across rounds, distinguish strong evidence from weak or confounded evidence, and account for epistemic uncertainty from limited knowledge and aleatoric uncertainty from inherent outcome variability. They will also study how different representations of prior experience (raw history, compaction, structured summaries, self-reflection) shape this behavior. A central open question is which failures can be addressed through how evidence is presented to the agent, and which require changing the model itself through post-training, and how to tell the two apart in a principled way. Intern candidates should have a strong interest in LLM agents, evaluation methodology, decision-making under uncertainty, and post-training. The ideal intern is comfortable conducting independent research, rapidly prototyping ideas, and collaborating with multidisciplinary scientists. This internship position is located in South San Francisco, on-site. The Opportunity Design and build a benchmark of real and controlled counterfactual decision points from multi-round scientific design campaigns Develop quantitative metrics for decision quality under varying evidence strength, uncertainty, and cost Characterize recurring agent failure modes and develop a principled method for deciding whether each is best addressed through harness or context changes or through post-training Explore post-training (e.g., supervised fine-tuning on historical trajectories and counterfactual examples) of smaller specialized models, and compare against prompting and context-engineering baselines on held-out targets Develop well-documented, reproducible evaluation and training code and curated datasets, and present results for potential publication at internal or external scientific conferences or workshops. Program Highlights Intensive 12-week, full-time (40 hours per week) paid internship. Program start dates are May/Summer (Summer). A stipend, based on location, will be provided to help alleviate costs associated with the internship . Who You Are Required Education Must be pursuing a PhD (enrolled student). Required Majors: Computer Science, Engineering, Statistics, Applied Mathematics, Computational Biology, Computational Chemistry, Physics, or related technical field. Required Skills Strong publication record or evidence of impactful research contributions (e.g., NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, TMLR). Proficiency in Python and modern ML frameworks (PyTorch, JAX, Hugging Face). Experience in at least one of the following areas: LLM agents, tool use, or agent evaluation and benchmarking. Post-training of language models (e.g., SFT, RLHF, RLVR). Sequential decision-making, Bayesian optimization, active learning, or uncertainty quantification. Preferred Skills Experience with agent memory and context management (e.g., summarization, compaction, self-reflection). Familiarity with protein design, structural biology, or drug discovery applications. Background in experimental design, causal reasoning, or counterfactual evaluation. Contributions to open-source ML frameworks or reproducible research environments. Excellent communication, collaboration, and interdisciplinary working skills Complements our culture and the standards that guide our daily behavior & decisions: Integrity, Courage, and Passion. Relocation benefits are not available for this job posting. The expected salary range for this position based on the primary location of California is $47.00-$52.00 per hour. Actual pay will be determined based on experience, qualifications, geographic location, and other job-related factors permitted by law. This position also qualifies for paid holiday time off benefits. Genentech

- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

The HR Lifecycle Operations team, endeavors to continuously build and deliver a seamless and positive employee experience across the whole employee lifecycle. We are dedicated to leading with care and have empathy in mind. We strive to develop efficient and simple people processes, systems, policies, and programs. We aspire to ensure employees feel heard and that they are given the appropriate resources and support needed to be effective and efficient during their careers. As a Benefits Specialist at TikTok, you will be supporting various employee benefits, including but not limited to Leave of Absence (LOA), healthcare benefits, and 401(k) retirement plan. The ideal candidate should have strong experience and knowledge in leave management, benefits operation and applicable compliance requirements per different types of plans, along with benefits vendor management and system optimization experience. 

The leave of Absence Analyst is responsible for auditing, analyzing, and visualizing leave-of-absence data (e.g., sick leave, parental leave, medical leave, unpaid leave) across the organization. By identifying trends, anomalies, and root causes of leave patterns, the role provides data-driven insights to optimize LOA policies, reduce excessive leave risks, improve workforce scheduling efficiency, and enhance employee well-being. This role collaborates closely with HR business partners, payroll teams, and department managers to align data analysis with business goals and ensure compliance with labor laws (e.g., FMLA in the U.S., statutory leave regulations in local markets).

 As a project intern, you will have the opportunity to engage in impactful short-term projects that provide you with a glimpse of professional real-world experience. You will gain practical skills through on-the-job learning in a fast-paced work environment and develop a deeper understanding of your career interests.

Applications will be reviewed on a rolling basis - we encourage you to apply early.

Responsibilities:
LOA Data Analysis & Trend Identification
- Conduct descriptive, diagnostic, and predictive analysis on LOA data:
- Descriptive: Track key LOA metrics (e.g., total LOA cases per quarter, average leave duration by type, department-wise leave rate, approval/rejection rate).
- Diagnostic: Investigate anomalies (e.g., sudden spikes in medical leave in a specific team, unusually long unpaid leave) and identify root causes (e.g., workplace stress, seasonal illnesses, policy gaps).
- Predictive: Forecast future leave demand (e.g., peak parental leave periods, Q4 sick leave trends) to support workforce planning.
- Analyze LOA impact on business operations (e.g., project delays due to key employee leave) and quantify associated costs (e.g., payroll for paid leave, hiring temporary staff).

Reporting & Visualization
- Develop regular (weekly/monthly/quarterly) LOA reports and dashboards for stakeholders (HR leaders, department managers, executives) using tools like Tableau, Power BI, Excel, or SQL.
- Translate complex data insights into clear, actionable recommendations (e.g., “Parental leave duration in Engineering is 20% longer than company average—suggest updating policy to align with team needs”).
- Present analysis results in meetings (e.g., quarterly HR reviews) and tailor communication to non-technical audiences.

Policy Optimization & Compliance Support
- Evaluate the effectiveness of existing LOA policies (e.g., “Is the new mental health leave policy reducing unplanned sick days?”) using data metrics and employee feedback.
- Provide data support for policy updates (e.g., adjusting paid leave limits, adding new leave types) to ensure alignment with labor laws (e.g., FMLA, EU Working Time Directive) and industry best practices.
- Collaborate with HRBP teams to communicate policy changes to employees and track adoption rates (e.g., how many employees use the new bereavement leave benefit).

Cross-Functional Collaboration
- Partner with payroll teams to ensure LOA data integrates with payroll processes (e.g., accurate calculation of paid leave wages, deduction for unpaid leave).
- Work with department managers to address team-specific LOA issues (e.g., high leave turnover in Customer Service) and develop targeted solutions (e.g., cross-training staff for backup).
- Liaise with tech teams to enhance HRIS functionality for LOA data tracking (e.g., adding custom fields for leave reason codes, automating leave request workflows). Minimum Qualifications:
  - Currently pursuing an Undergraduate/Masters in Data Science, Statistics, Human Resources Management, Business Analytics, or a related field. 
  - Proficiency in data querying tools (SQL, HQL) to extract LOA data from HRIS/payroll systems.
  - Experience with data visualization tools (Tableau, Power BI, Qlik) and spreadsheet software (Advanced Excel: PivotTables, VLOOKUP, macros).
  - Basic knowledge of statistical analysis (e.g., trend analysis, correlation, forecasting) and data cleaning techniques.
  - Understanding of leave-of-absence types (sick, parental, medical, unpaid, statutory leave) and relevant labor laws (e.g., FMLA, local statutory leave regulations).
  - Knowledge of HR operations (payroll, workforce planning, employee relations) to contextualize LOA data insights.
 
Preferred Qualifications: 
  - Experience in HR analytics, workforce analytics, or data analysis (preferably with a focus on leave management, payroll, or employee relations).
  - Certification in data analytics (e.g., Google Data Analytics Professional Certificate, Tableau Desktop Specialist) or HR (e.g., SHRM-CP, PHR).
  - Experience working in a global organization (familiarity with US leave policies and multi-country data standards).
  - Familiarity with HRIS platforms (Workday, ADP) is highly preferred.

By submitting an application for this role, you accept and agree to our global applicant privacy policy, which may be accessed here: https://careers.tiktok.com/legal/privacy

### Job description

Data Scientist Data Scientist GenLogs is a transportation-technology company building the next generation of truck intelligence. Through a nationwide network of sensors and proprietary data, we deliver real-time, high-fidelity insights into freight movement for commercial supply-chain customers and public-sector agencies. Our mission is to strengthen America’s logistics backbone, combat freight fraud and cargo theft, and provide near-instantaneous visibility into commercial motor vehicle activity across major freight corridors. By operating at the intersection of edge sensing, computer vision, AI-driven analytics, and large-scale field deployment, GenLogs is transforming how transportation data is captured, secured, and commercialized. ABOUT THE DATA TEAM The Data Science team at GenLogs transforms raw observational data from the our sensor network into high-value intelligence used by law-enforcement agencies, regulators, ports, and private-sector freight operators. We build models, analytics, and measurement frameworks that enable vehicle detection, entity resolution, behavioral insights, fraud and theft indicators, compliance signals, and network-wide operational performance metrics. Our work sits at the center of the freight intelligence platform, shaping how billions of roadside observations become actionable information. We partner closely with Engineering and Product to deploy algorithms at scale and with Go-to-Market teams to define customer-facing analyses that drive real operational outcomes. The team blends statistical rigor, ML capability, and domain expertise to create a new standard for freight intelligence in the United States. ABOUT THE JOB You’re a problem solver at heart. You thrive at the intersection of engineering, math, and machine learning , and you’re motivated by questions that don’t have obvious answers. You bring a background in engineering, computer science, physics, applied math, or another hard science discipline, and you enjoy applying that technical foundation to real-world ML challenges. You are energized by ambiguity, obsessed with understanding how complex systems behave, and capable of breaking down big problems into tractable iterations. You ask great questions, validate assumptions with data, and are relentless in your pursuit of signal over noise. WHAT YOU’LL DO Build machine-learning systems that power some of the most advanced logistics intelligence products in the industry Analyze large, noisy datasets from cameras, OCR, detections, and geospatial pipelines to uncover actionable patterns Design and evaluate algorithms for truck re-identification, geospatial clustering, equipment classification, OCR text labeling , anomaly detection, and more Collaborate with engineering and data engineering teams to scale models from prototype to production Work closely with product teams to deeply understand customer needs and translate them into modeling and analytics initiatives Apply scientific thinking to continuously test, iterate, and refine approaches as new data becomes available REQUIRED QUALIFICATIONS 2–5 years of professional experience in Data Science, Machine Learning, or Software Engineering Technical foundation in engineering, physics, math, computer science , or related applied fields Experience deploying or building models using: Machine learning fundamentals (classification, clustering, time-series, anomaly detection) Computer vision (OCR, object detection, embeddings) Geospatial data analysis (mapping, clustering, location intelligence) Association/sequence pattern mining , feature engineering, or algorithm development Experience working with cloud-based data tooling (Snowflake, AWS) — not required but nice to have Strong programming skills in Python and comfort with modern data/ML libraries (PyTorch, Pandas, Scikit-learn, etc.) Comfort working with real-world messy datasets (sensor data, imagery, telematics, transactional freight data) WHO WILL SUCCEED HERE You will love this role if you are: Relentlessly curious — you ask “why?” repeatedly until you reach the root Technically fearless — not afraid to dive into large datasets, new ML techniques, or unfamiliar codebases Impact-driven — you want your models to power real, high-stakes decisions in a massive industry Comfortable with ambiguity — our data is large, messy, and evolving, and that excites you Collaborative — you enjoy working with engineers, data teams, and product stakeholders to deliver real customer value US SALARY RANGE GenLogs establishes compensation based on role, level, experience, and location. Salary bands are benchmarked against high-growth technology companies and adjusted for market conditions. Equity grants are included in most full-time offers to ensure every team member participates in the company’s long-term value creation. A recruiter will provide a precise range during the hiring process. BENEFITS Healthcare (US based only) Employer-covered comprehensive medical, dental, and vision plans Employer contribution towards premiums of optional higher-end plans Time Off Unlimited PTO Sick leave Company holidays (GenLogs observes all US Government holidays) Flexible leave for caregiving and medical needs Family Support Paid parental leave Professional Development Budget availability for approved professional development courses, certifications, and training Travel Support 100% travel reimbursement for all approved company travel and spending Retirement Savings 401(k) plan (US based employees) A recruiter can provide more detail about the specific compensation and benefits associated with this role. About the Institute of Foundation Models
The Institute of Foundation Models is a research lab at Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence (MBZUAI) dedicated to building, understanding, and responsibly deploying foundation models. Our researchers and engineers work across model training, evaluation, and systems. We share our work through publications, technical reports, and open-source releases, and help train the next generation of AI researchers and builders.
 The Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) team studies how foundation models can contribute to training and evaluating their successors. A model might construct a coding task, write a verifier, or use execution feedback to revise a solution. The research question is whether those contributions improve the next model’s ability to solve unfamiliar problems, and whether that model can, in turn, produce better training tasks and feedback. As a Research Scientist, you will shape this research agenda and lead projects from an initial hypothesis through large-scale experiments. You will design learning algorithms, build environments that provide meaningful feedback, and study how gains and errors accumulate over successive training rounds. The role combines independent research with hands-on engineering across data generation, post-training, and evaluation. Develop algorithms for recursive self-improvement. Design and test methods for models to propose tasks, attempt solutions, learn from feedback, and select experiences for subsequent training rounds. Build tasks and environments with verifiable outcomes. Develop agentic tasks in domains such as coding, program analysis, and tool use. Design execution-based verifiers and investigate what happens when a verifier accepts an incorrect solution or rejects a valid one. Study how to select training data as models improve. Investigate which tasks produce the greatest learning gains at each stage of training and how to maintain coverage as earlier tasks become easier. Measure improvement across generations. Design held-out evaluations, ablations, and compute-matched baselines that isolate the contribution of model-generated tasks and feedback. Diagnose failure modes such as reward hacking, declining task diversity, and errors that compound across rounds. Scale promising methods. Work with research engineers to integrate generation, verification, and training into distributed post-training systems. Quantify the trade-offs among model capability, feedback quality, and computational cost. Communicate and share research. Publish findings, contribute reproducible code and evaluations for public release, and build collaborations within MBZUAI and across the research community. MSc, MEng, or PhD in machine learning, computer science, or a related field, or equivalent research experience. Compensation
$150,000 – $450,000/year + discretionary performance bonus + benefits
The posted salary range represents the company’s good faith estimate of the base compensation for this position upon hire. The actual base compensation offered may vary within this range depending on individual qualifications, including but not limited to relevant skills, experience, education, certifications, and specific business needs. In addition to base salary, total compensation includes an annual discretionary performance bonus and comprehensive benefits.
Benefits Include
Comprehensive medical, dental, and vision benefits
Bonus
401K Plan
Generous paid time off, sick leave and holidays
Paid Parental Leave
Employee Assistance Program
Life insurance and disability
Professional Experience
Minimum
Three or more years of hands-on research experience in machine learning, including substantial work in reinforcement learning, foundation model post-training, program synthesis, or learning from model-generated data.
Experience independently formulating a research question, implementing a method, and designing experiments that distinguish among plausible explanations for a result.
Strong Python skills and experience implementing learning algorithms or evaluation pipelines in a deep learning framework such as PyTorch or JAX.
Practical experience training or evaluating models using feedback from an environment, a verifier, human supervision, or another model.
Demonstrated research or engineering contributions through publications, technical reports, open-source software, or deployed systems.
Ability to communicate findings clearly and collaborate with researchers and engineers across disciplines.
Preferred Skills
Experience with self-play, curriculum learning, meta-RL, expert iteration, or iterative training on synthetic data.
Experience building coding agents, executable task environments, programmatic verifiers, or formal verification tools.
Familiarity with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, reward modeling, or learning under noisy or systematically biased feedback.
Experience evaluating multi-step agents, detecting benchmark contamination, or measuring generalization to unfamiliar tasks.
Experience running distributed training or inference on large clusters and diagnosing bottlenecks across rollout generation, environment execution, and optimization.
A record of publications in leading machine learning venues or contributions to widely used open-source training and evaluation tools.

### Job description

ABOUT THE ROLE

Join an early-stage AI team developing neuro-symbolic foundation models for high-stakes fields such as healthcare, finance, and law. You will own major engineering workstreams from research through enterprise deployment, building systems designed for transparent reasoning and reliable outputs.


WHAT YOU'LL DO

 - Design and implement novel neural architectures in PyTorch, balancing accuracy, latency, model capacity, and hardware constraints.

 - Build models that extract information from unstructured data and support transparent reasoning through constrained decoding, inference-time search, and principled abstention.

 - Develop pre-training, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning pipelines for multi-node GPU clusters, including evaluation and hyperparameter sweeps.

 - Evaluate hallucinations with judge models, blinded holdouts, and regression gates, and create synthetic data checked by model critics.

 - Optimize low-latency inference and structured knowledge lookup at scale, integrating clinical terminology and enterprise data.

 - Contribute to technical roadmap decisions and help grow the engineering team.


WHAT WE'RE LOOKING FOR

 - An MS, MTech, or PhD in computer science, electrical engineering, or a related field, or an equivalent research record.

 - Research experience designing, training, and evaluating neural models end to end, demonstrated through publications, a thesis, or notable open-source work.

 - Hands-on experience building foundation models and novel architectures from scratch in PyTorch, as a technical individual contributor.

 - Experience across model development, including pre-training, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning; familiarity with FSDP, DeepSpeed, vLLM, or SGLang is valuable.

 - Experience with large-model reinforcement learning, structured decoding, inference engines, knowledge graphs, or information extraction.

 - Biomedical data experience, such as clinical terminologies, or experience delivering enterprise systems in mission-critical settings.

 - A record of shipping models whose outputs are checked against formal schemas, verifiers, or knowledge graphs.


COMPENSATION & BENEFITS

Base salary is $200,000 to $400,000 annually, adjusted for location. Founding engineer equity is 0.5% to 2%. Visa sponsorship is available, including O-1A, H-1B transfer, and TN.


LOCATION

Hybrid in San Jose, California.

This position may be filled at multiple education and training levels, including candidates with a bachelor’s degree in biology or a related scientific field (with research experience), candidates with a master’s degree, and candidates with a doctoral (PhD) degree. Reporting to the Principal Investigator and senior laboratory personnel, the Research Associate will carry responsibility for the operational conduct of specific research activities within the laboratory’s active portfolio. Core responsibilities include participant recruitment and enrollment, obtaining informed consent in accordance with IRB-approved procedures, executing study visits across the laboratory’s human subjects protocols, performing physiological monitoring and environmental exposure assessments, collecting and processing biospecimens, maintaining accurate study records, contributing to protocol refinement and procedural documentation, and supporting less-senior laboratory staff as needed. The Research Associate works at a level of independence that reflects practical familiarity with the laboratory’s protocols and standards. The role offers substantive engagement with clinical research methodology, biomarker science, physiological monitoring, and the operational discipline of federally funded clinical research. ABOUT NU

Nu serves more than 143 million customers, guided by a mission to fight complexity and empower people. The company has been leading an industry transformation through innovative products and human-centered services.

 

Proprietary technology and data at scale power Nu’s digital platform, built to promote financial access, advancement, and transparency. Its business model thrives on customer love and lower costs, feeding a flywheel of growth and profitability.

Visit our Institutional Page https://www.nu.com/2026-en


ABOUT THE ROLE 

At Nu, you’ll work with a view of customer behavior that few companies have. Many companies predict what people might buy from page likes and video views. Nu sees what customers actually spend on, through their financial transactions. nuFormer https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23267 is the first banking transformer foundation model used in production to improve financial decisions for more than 100 million customers across multiple countries. Its teams turn this data into a deeper understanding of customers and into models that make a real difference in people’s financial lives. You’ll work alongside colleagues from many backgrounds, tackling hard problems where artificial intelligence meets financial services.



YOU’LL BE RESPONSIBLE FOR 

Leading a team who improve the data behind nuFormer. Your team will explore how better data quality, richer financial signals, and new ways of preparing and using data can expand what the model can do. You’ll grow your engineers, set the team’s technical direction, and turn research findings into production improvements. You’ll also stay hands-on in technical decisions, including reviewing designs and contributing code when the path forward is unclear.

 - Guides the team’s research and engineering work on the data behind nuFormer, and delivers measurable production improvements to model performance and data quality.

 - Gives regular feedback, discusses performance and career goals, and coaches each engineer toward their next stage of growth.

 - Translates company goals into engineering priorities and a medium-term technical roadmap. Coordinates with partner teams and updates the roadmap as needs change.

 - Makes architecture decisions, reviews system designs, and contributes code to solve complex problems, from design through implementation.

 - Spots and resolves issues such as poor data quality, declining model performance, unstable training, and infrastructure bottlenecks. Reviews models for quality, accuracy, and bias.

 - Reports progress and priorities to leaders, explains technical tradeoffs in plain language, and brings relevant new research into production

 


WHAT WE'RE LOOKING FOR SOMEONE WHO HAS

Required

 - A master’s degree or PhD in engineering, computer science, or a related technical field.

 - Experience developing software in production.

 - Experience leading the design and improvement of machine learning systems, including data processing, model evaluation, deployment, debugging, and fine-tuning.

 - Experience in technical leadership, including directly managing machine learning teams in large-scale environments, such as major technology companies or frontier research labs.

 - Experience setting technical strategy and roadmaps that cover the full machine learning lifecycle, from bringing data into the system to serving models in production.

 - Experience working with product, data science, and research partners to define problems suited to machine learning and deliver measurable results.

 - A record of hiring, developing, and retaining machine learning engineers, and of building teams that make progress amid uncertainty.
   

Nice to Have

 - Experience with advanced deep learning architectures, including transformer-based models.

 - Experience running data-focused machine learning research on how data quality, selection, and representation affect model performance, and applying the findings to training data and systems.

 - Experience applying techniques from published research to production systems.

 - Experience carrying out responsible AI practices, such as risk assessments, bias mitigation, and model quality reviews.

 - Examples of using AI tools to change a workflow, with measurable gains in efficiency or quality.

 - Ongoing practice building your own AI skills, such as writing prompts, designing the context given to models, or coordinating multiple AI agents

Location

 - Miami, United States

 - Washington DC, United States

Work model

Hybrid 2-3 times/week: Our hybrid work model brings us to the office at least twice a week, on strategic days designed to maximize team connection and collaboration. For more details, visit https://building.nubank.com/nu-hybrid-work-model/

 


OUR BENEFITS 

 - Total compensation includes base salary, RSUs and benefits. Base salary range: $216k -  $324k. Location-Specific Disclosures
   
   • Opportunity of earning equity at Nu
   • Medical Insurance
   • Dental and Vision Insurance
   • Life Insurance and AD&D
   • Extended maternity and paternity leaves
   • Nucleo - Our learning platform of courses
   • NuLanguage - Our language learning program
   • NuCare - Our mental health and wellness assistance program
   • 401K Saving Plans - Health Saving Account and Flexible Spending Account
   • Work-from-home Allowance
   • Relocation Assistance Package, if applicable

Our recruitment process may involve the use of artificial intelligence–enabled tools, such as automated interview transcription and analysis, to support the evaluation process. Artificial intelligence is not used to make final hiring decisions; all decisions are made by human reviewers.

To maintain a consistent and fair process for every candidate, Nu does not provide individualized technical feedback. See how our policy works here https://script.google.com/a/macros/nubank.com.br/s/AKfycbwvrewE58oGSbRWIVv8CyCWN68GLxpPVsfYYXv-ZHg94QFsTJmi82rF1x0yt-OTs2W0DQ/exec

### Job description

Job Classification: Investment Management - Investments As the Global Asset Management business of Prudential, we’re always looking for ways to improve financial services. We’re passionate about making a meaningful impact - touching the lives of millions and solving financial challenges in an ever-changing world. We also believe talent is key to achieving our vision and are intentional about building a culture on respect and collaboration. When you join PGIM, you’ll unlock a motivating and impactful career – all while growing your skills and advancing your profession at one of the world’s leading global asset managers! If you’re not afraid to think differently and challenge the status quo, come and be a part of a dedicated team that’s investing in your future by shaping tomorrow today. At PGIM, You Can! The PGIM Quantitative Solutions (PQS) trading team is responsible for the accurate and timely execution of trades submitted by portfolio managers. Trading must be conducted in a way that meets best execution requirements expected by PQS’s investment management clients. In addition to execution responsibilities, the trade team monitors the performance of trades and trading counterparties; ensures that trades are accurately filled in a timely manner; sees that trade results are quickly reconciled and incorporated into portfolio management and accounting systems; and evaluates brokers, counterparties and trading technologies. The team works very closely with portfolio management and reports daily on trading performance and market conditions. Most equity trading involves large baskets of securities and may involve balanced, or unbalanced trades. Although the team’s primary focus is on global equity trading, it is also responsible for trading a variety of other securities around the world including a variety of derivatives and some fixed income instruments. Most trading is accomplished within a normal business day, but as international segment is a fast-growing business, overnight and weekend trading will likely become necessary. What you will do: Trading equities, futures, options and other financial instruments on US and International portfolios. Monitor contributions and withdrawals and futures positions in equity portfolios. Prepare baskets for agency and principal trading. Work block trades. Prepare detail analysis of international trades; run individual or aggregate pre-trade and post trade analyses; working overnight and weekends may become necessary. Reconcile post trade performance and monitor individual broker performance. Monitor developments affecting broker capabilities and business stability. Monitor and report on market conditions. Monitor developments in trade execution theory and practice, including new execution venues and tools used by institutional trade desks. Keep abreast of academic and industry literature relevant to trading. Collaborate with senior management to provide feedback on market conditions, client needs, and emerging trends to shape future offerings. What you will bring: Bachelor’s Degree, Math or Science, quantitative skills preferred. 3-5 years working in a domestic/international trading environment trading equities, options and futures. Experience with various trading algorithms and platforms. Strong understanding of investment management processes, equity and fixed income markets and procedures and controls related to trading, portfolio accounting and portfolio administration. This includes a strong understanding of trade entry, settlement and reconciliation processes. Knowledge of international equity and currency markets and institutions. Working knowledge of modern portfolio theory, stock market microstructure. The incumbent should have a strong understanding of the basics of trade cost measurement including VWAP and implementation shortfall. Strong quantitative and analytical skills. Some experience with computing languages and database structure is helpful. Excellent communicator, negation, and relationship building skills. What will set you apart? Programming skills Advanced skills in Excel and capable of working with large data sets. SWAP/FX Trading Experience AI Related Training Buffered ETF Trading Experience Knowledge of Algorithmic Trading Strategies PGIM believes in the Power of a Hybrid work arrangement. The Power of Hybrid is both the flexibility of work from home, as well as the networking and career development of in-person work with colleagues through three weekly anchor days. Note: Prudential is required by state-specific laws to include the salary range for this role when hiring a resident in applicable locations. The salary range for this role is from $165,000.00 - $175,000.00 USD. Specific pricing for the role may vary within the above range based on many factors including geographic location, candidate experience, and skills. Roles may also be eligible for additional compensation and/or benefits. Eligibility to participate in a discretionary annual incentive program is subject to the rules governing the program, whereby an award, if any, depends on various factors including, without limitation, individual and organizational performance. #LI-Hybrid #LI-CR1 What we offer you: Market competitive base salaries, with a yearly bonus potential at every level. Medical, dental, vision, life insurance, disability insurance, Paid Time Off (PTO), and leave of absences, such as parental and military leave. 401(k) plan with company match (up to 4%). Company-funded pension plan. Wellness Programs including up to $1,600 a year for reimbursement of items purchased to support personal wellbeing needs. Work/Life Resources to help support topics such as parenting, housing, senior care, finances, pets, legal matters, education, emotional and mental health, and career development. Education Benefit to help finance traditional college enrollment toward obtaining an approved degree and many accredited certificate programs. Employee Stock Purchase Plan: Shares can be purchased at 85% of the lower of two prices (Beginning or End of the purchase period). Eligibility to participate in a discretionary annual incentive program is subject to the rules governing the program, whereby an award, if any, depends on various factors including, without limitation, individual and organizational performance. To find out more about our Total Rewards package, visit Work Life Balance | Prudential Careers. Some of the above benefits may not apply to part-time employees scheduled to work less than 20 hours per week. Prudential Financial, Inc. of the United States is not affiliated with Prudential plc. which is headquartered in the United Kingdom. Prudential

### Job description

About InterDigital InterDigital is a global research and development company focused primarily on wireless, video, artificial intelligence (“AI”), and related technologies. We design and develop foundational technologies that enable connected, immersive experiences in a broad range of communications and entertainment products and services. We license our innovations worldwide to companies providing such products and services, including makers of wireless communications devices, consumer electronics, IoT devices, cars and other motor vehicles, and providers of cloud-based services such as video streaming. As a leader in wireless technology, our engineers have designed and developed a wide range of innovations that are used in wireless products and networks, from the earliest digital cellular systems to 5G and today’s most advanced Wi-Fi technologies. We are also a leader in video processing and video encoding/decoding technology, with a significant AI research effort that intersects with both wireless and video technologies. Founded in 1972, InterDigital is listed on Nasdaq. InterDigital is a registered trademark of InterDigital, Inc. For more information, visit: www.interdigital.com . We foster a collaborative, innovation-driven culture where researchers translate advanced theory into impactful technologies, standards contributions, and real-world solutions. Summary We are seeking a highly motivated Ph.D. student to join our Wireless Research and Innovation team. This role focuses on next-generation wireless systems integrated with AI/ML, including 6G research, and AI-native communication systems. The ideal candidate combines strong theoretical foundations with hands-on simulation experience in Python and MATLAB. The candidate will contribute to advanced research, AI-native wireless systems and simulations, standards-oriented innovation, publications, and patentable technologies. Key Responsibilities Conduct advanced research in wireless communication systems in Physical layer, and AI/ML design Work on internal technology development efforts and ensure alignment with broader project objectives Design and evaluate AI/ML-driven wireless algorithms (e.g., semantic communications, wireless foundation models, token communications) Develop and implement link-level and system-level simulations using tools such as Python, Sionna, MATLAB or equivalent frameworks Analyze and optimize system performance using theoretical and data-driven approaches Contribute to technical reports, publications, and patent filings Contribute to the generation of pre-standard inputs and industry publications in areas of expertise Collaborate with cross-functional research teams across wireless, AI, and standards Required Qualifications Pursuing Ph.D. (3rd year or later) in Electrical Engineering, Computer Engineering, Computer Science, or a closely related field. Strong foundation in the following areas: Wireless communications Signal processing Solid knowledge of AI/ML techniques and their application to wireless communication Experience with simulation and programming tools, such as: Sionna, Python, MATLAB, or similar languages Machine learning frameworks (e.g., PyTorch, GPU) Demonstrated ability to translate theory into working simulations Preferred Qualifications Hands-on experience with System-level or link-level simulators (e.g., Sionna or equivalent) Research track record including publications, patents, or technical contributions Familiarity with cellular standards (4G/5G/6G evolution) Experience in AI-native wireless systems, semantic communications, wireless foundation models, token communications, etc. What You Bring Curiosity and passion for pushing the boundaries of wireless and AI convergence Ability to balance theoretical rigor with practical implementation Strong analytical, problem-solving, and communication skills A collaborative mindset aligned with a global research environment Locations: Conshohocken, PA or Melville, NY Why Join InterDigital Work at the forefront of 6G, AI-driven networks, and future connectivity paradigms Contribute to technologies that influence global standards and billions of devices Engage in an environment that values innovation, intellectual property, and research excellence Collaborate with world-class experts across wireless, video, and AI domains InterDigital

### Job description

We build. We create impact. Vusion is seeking a detail-oriented Product Analyst to support the accuracy, consistency, and integrity of product information within our Data & AI solutions. Combining product data analysis, AI-assisted classification validation, research, and client support, this role helps deliver reliable product intelligence for leading U.S. retailers. As an individual contributor, you'll manage high volumes of product information, investigate classification exceptions, resolve data discrepancies, and collaborate with Data, Product, and Client Solutions teams to maintain quality and drive continuous improvement. You will make a positive impact by being responsible for: Reviewing AI-assisted product classifications, validating accurate results, and correcting errors based on established hierarchy standards. Researching ambiguous or low-confidence grocery, retail, and consumer packaged goods (CPG) products using product attributes, packaging, and approved sources. Identifying duplicate, inconsistent, or incomplete product records and maintaining accurate, auditable classification changes. Independently resolving routine classification exceptions and escalating complex cases with supporting information. Supporting the classification and onboarding of new products and retailer datasets. Responding to client inquiries, clearly explaining classification decisions, and coordinating resolutions with internal subject matter experts. Identifying recurring classification issues and recommending improvements to documentation, business rules, and processes. Maintaining classification accuracy, productivity, and timely client responses in accordance with established quality standards and service-level expectations. Qualifications we're looking for Experience 1–3 years of relevant experience in data analysis, data operations, retail merchandising, product information management, catalog management, or related field. Experience working with structured datasets, identifying inconsistencies, and maintaining data accuracy while following established processes. Preferred Experience Familiarity with U.S. grocery, retail, and CPG products and how product categories are organized. Experience with product catalogs, product attributes, merchandising data, or product hierarchy management. Skills Proficiency in Microsoft Excel, structured data, and data validation. Strong research, analytical, and problem-solving skills with exceptional attention to detail. Clear written and verbal communication skills, including the ability to explain findings and decisions to external clients. We are looking for a Finance Data Analyst Intern who combines an understanding of accounting and finance with strong technical skills. This role is ideal for someone who enjoys working with financial data, building automated workflows, connecting software applications, and developing solutions that improve business processes. Proficiency in at least one programming or data tool, such as Python, SQL, JavaScript, or similar. Strong proficiency in Excel or Google Sheets. Exposure to automation platforms, webhooks, connectors, or AI-powered tools is highly desirable. Education We welcome candidates from multiple academic backgrounds, including: Accounting and Finance Computer Science Information Systems / Management Information Systems (MIS) Data Analytics / Business Analytics Economics or related quantitative fields. Candidates pursuing a technology-focused degree with accounting or finance coursework, or a finance/accounting degree with demonstrated programming and automation skills, are particularly encouraged to apply. Graduation: December 2027 is preferred; May 2027 or later will also be considered. Who You Are Technically Strong: You understand how APIs, software integrations, and automated workflows operate. Financially Curious: You understand basic accounting concepts and are interested in how financial systems work. Analytical: You enjoy working with data, identifying inconsistencies, and solving problems. A Builder: You like developing solutions rather than relying entirely on manual processes. Detail-Oriented: You recognize the importance of accuracy in financial reporting and data management. Proactive: You take initiative, ask questions, and follow through on assignments. Adaptable: You enjoy learning new technologies and working in a fast-paced startup environment. What You Will Gain Direct mentorship and exposure to the Controller and Finance team. Partnering with engineering, architecture, product, and platform teams, you'll help deliver scalable, reliable solutions and modernize the systems that support them. What You’ll Do Design, build, test, and support scalable software and data solutions within AWS. Develop and maintain data ingestion, transformation, and processing pipelines using AWS Glue. Build data solutions using Apache Iceberg and Amazon S3-based data architectures. Design and develop solutions using PostgreSQL and Amazon Aurora MySQL. Own database and data-performance optimization, including query tuning, indexing strategies, execution-plan analysis, schema optimization, and troubleshooting high-volume workloads. Build reliable integrations between applications, APIs, operational databases, and enterprise data platforms. Help modernize legacy application, database, and ETL patterns into cloud-native architectures. Experience with Amazon S3 and AWS-based data architectures. Strong SQL skills and proficiency with Python, Java, or another modern programming language. Adaptive Communication Formulates strategies to be used to convey complex information about services, products, systems, or processes to targeted audiences; communicates and liaises between technical and non-technical audiences. ### Job description

The TikTok Commerce Ads team is dedicated to blending advertiser focus with technical expertise. Our mission is to enhance commerce ads—both open-loop and closed-loop—to establish TikTok as an essential growth engine for businesses of all sizes and a top destination for consumers to discover and buy products, content, and services. We are crucial to driving TikTok's ad revenue and are committed to developing the next-generation web ad solutions for the platform.

We are looking for talented individuals to join us for an internship. Our internship program offers students hands-on experience, industry exposure, and opportunities to apply their knowledge to real-world challenges while building a strong foundation for personal and professional growth.

Interns will gain practical experience, explore potential career paths, and participate in social events, learning programs, and development workshops alongside industry professionals.

Candidates may apply to a maximum of two positions across Our Company and its affiliates globally. Applications will be considered in the order they are submitted.

Applications are reviewed on a rolling basis, so we encourage you to apply early. Please clearly state your availability in your resume, including your start and end dates.

Responsibilities:
- Build highly scalable machine learning systems and state-of-the-art machine learning models to improve ads ranking quality and optimize advertisers' marketing strategies. Examples include but are not limited to click through rate prediction, conversion rate prediction, intelligent format selection and user journey optimization.
- Explore, develop and experiment with new features to improve model accuracy.
- Understand ads platform objectives and take full advantage of modern machine learning to improve ads relevance, quality, and quantity delivered to end-users.
- Collaborate with Product Managers, Designers, and other disciplines to explore the next generation of shopping experiences on TikTok. Minimum Qualifications:
- Currently pursuing a Bachelor's or above degree in Computer Science, Computer Engineering, or a related technical discipline.
- Solid programming skills, including but not limited to: Go, C/C++, Python. Familiar with basic data structure and algorithms. Familiar with Linux development environment.
- Good analytical thinking capability. Have essential knowledge and skills in statistics.
- Good theoretical grounding in the machine and deep learning concepts and techniques (CNN/RNN/LSTM, etc.).
- Familiar with the architecture and implementation of at least one mainstream machine learning programming framework (TensorFlow/PyTorch/MXNet), familiar with its architecture and implementation mechanism.
- Must be able to commit to a 12-week full-time work period during Summer 2027.

Preferred Qualifications:
- Good understanding in one of the following domains: ads bidding & auction, ads quality control, and online advertising systems (familiar with one or more of these terms: CPC/CPM, CTR/CVR, Ranking /Targeting, Conversion/Budget, Campaign/Creative, Demand/Inventory, DSP/RTB).
- Experience in resource management and task scheduling with large-scale distributed software (such as Spark and TensorFlow).
- Relevant work or research experiences in search and recommendation.

Prior experience with high-temporal resolution imaging techniques. Familiarity with the handling and study of wastewater treatment and resource recovery. Experience mentoring or supervising graduate or undergraduate researchers. ## 41. [MAYBE] Teza Technologies | Quantitative Developer
- Link: https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/teza-technologies/508534c6-ec5e-4b50-9d84-84d812102853
- Location: Austin United States Yerevan
- Posted: Nov 24 12:33 ET
- Scanner fit: ~52 | Min YOE: None | H-1B (certified LCAs, Oct 2024-Dec 2025): 3 in data/analytics/finance occupations
- Type: full-time
- Title scope: core | Has: postgres, python, sql, backtest, git, pipeline, portfolio, quantitative, research, trading | Lacks: airflow, java
- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

About the role

Teza has built a new intent generation platform and strategy building framework for mid-frequency trading, written in-house from scratch. It is the layer that turns research into production strategies: the framework defines how a strategy is expressed, the platform computes target positions and generates trading intents.



One strategy already trades on the platform in production at seconds-scale frequency. Next, all of our existing strategies move onto it, and completely new strategies get built directly on top of it. This role is central to that effort.



Your job is to implement strategies on the platform, build the data pipelines that feed them, and implement the execution algorithms that trade them. The role covers the whole path from raw data to orders in the market, and you own the full lifecycle: the engineer who implements a strategy also understands, and often builds, its upstream data and downstream execution.



The data our strategies trade on, by the numbers:

 - 3 PB of historical market data, growing by 1.5 TB compressed every day

 - 1 PB of raw historical vendor data, growing by ~150 GB every day

 - 120 TB of processed, query-ready data in historical storage

 - Thousands of scheduled jobs: run by cron today, actively migrating to Apache Airflow

 - Alternative data delivered directly into the real-time feeds of live trading strategies

The firm is looking for outstanding technical skills, strong attention to detail, and a desire to own strategies end to end, from the data they read to the orders they send.



Location

Austin, TX / Yerevan, Armenia (in-office requirement)



Key Responsibilities

 - Implement strategies on the new framework: migrate the existing ones, validating behavioral parity for each one, and build completely new ones.

 - Work directly with Portfolio Managers and Quantitative Researchers: turn their research into production strategies, and serve as the authority on how those strategies compute and trade.

 - Implement execution algorithms that turn a strategy's intents into orders, and measure their execution quality.

 - Design and onboard new data sources into our warehouse, and build the pipelines that deliver them to research and to live strategies.

 - Build automated systems for data cleansing, anomaly detection, monitoring, and alerting, ensuring bad data never reaches a strategy.

 - Shape the framework through the strategies you build on it: identify bottlenecks and friction points, and spearhead the necessary enhancements.

 - Support your strategies, pipelines, and execution algorithms in production, where they trade real capital.



What we're building right now

 - Migration of all existing strategies onto the new platform, alongside completely new strategies built natively on it.

 - Execution algorithms for the strategies running on the platform.

 - Core framework capabilities underpinning the strategies you implement: parallel compute, data locality, and caching of intermediate results across strategies, backtests, and live runs.

 - A bitemporal data store, designed and written in-house from scratch. Every dataset answers both "what did we know then?" and "what do we know now?", which is what lets researchers trust a backtest.

 - A Python 3.14 migration of a large, long-lived codebase.

 - Adoption of the latest Apache Airflow: writing DAGs for the thousands of jobs moving off cron.

 - Pipelines for market data and alternative data: everything from exchange feeds to weather.

 - Real-time delivery: alternative data flows straight into strategies' live feeds. Your work is part of the live trading path.

 - CI/CD for all of it, in GitHub Actions.



Our Stack

Python and Java · Apache Airflow · Slurm · NATS · PostgreSQL · MongoDB · S3 · NFS · GitHub Actions



Basic Requirements

 - Strong software engineering in Python on Unix/Linux, with a track record of shipping production code that others depend on.

 - Experience implementing quantitative models or trading strategies in code, and making numerical, data-intensive code correct and fast.

 - Experience building and running data pipelines: ingestion, cleaning, scheduling, and monitoring.

 - Strong SQL, including query optimization and performance tuning, and a solid grasp of data modeling.

 - Comfort working with large time-series datasets, and the rigor to prevent look-ahead bias.

 - Solid computer science fundamentals: data structures and algorithms.



Nice to have

 - Experience at a systematic trading firm, implementing strategies inside a backtesting or strategy framework.

 - Experience implementing execution algorithms, and familiarity with market microstructure, order types, and transaction cost analysis.

 - Java (part of our platform is written in it).

 - Apache Airflow or similar workflow orchestration tools.

 - Experience with Slurm or another HPC scheduler.

 - Experience with on-premises data infrastructure.



Benefits

 - Health, vision and dental insurance

 - Flexible sick time policy

### Job description

Principal Investigator, Dr. Joshua Levy, is looking for a Research Associate I to join the team! Cedars-Sinai is committed to pushing the frontiers of medicine and discovering new technologies and innovations that will change how we diagnose and treat disease. As an international leader in biomedical research, we translate discoveries into successful treatments with global impact. With your help, we can change the future of healthcare! The Levy Lab at Cedars-Sinai Medical Center is dedicated to developing and validating innovative digital pathology and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies for histopathology and clinical implementation . Our primary goal is to establish the credibility and adoptability of these technologies by integrating them into clinical workflows and ensuring they meet both algorithmic and practical stakeholder readiness criteria through rigorous validation and stress testing. Our research focuses on applying AI, digital pathology, and spatial multiomics to tackle key challenges in anatomic pathology. Notable research areas from the Levy Lab include: Virtual RNA inference methods: Predict spatial gene expression from histology to provide deeper insights into tumor biology. Spatial multimodal analysis: Explore metal-based pathways in tumor progression to improve diagnostic accuracy and guide treatment strategies. Urine cytology AI algorithm: Detect abnormal cells in cytology specimens containing hundreds of thousands of cells, offering a noninvasive diagnostic adjunct for high-volume bladder cancer screening and surveillance. Intraoperative margin assessment tool: Build a real-time 3D model of the tumor from dozens of tissue sections within 70 seconds after scanning, helping surgeons assess tumor margins and ensure complete removal of cancer during surgery. The Levy Lab fosters an interdisciplinary, collaborative environment that bridges basic science and clinical research. We emphasize reverse translation studies , where clinical insights inform basic science discoveries. Lab members are encouraged to lead independent, biomedical research projects, work closely with clinicians, and gain exposure to a blend of computational, biomedical, and clinical research perspectives . To learn more, please visit Levy Lab at Cedars-Sinai . Are you ready to be a part of breakthrough research? Working under direct supervision, the Research Associate I perform a variety of routine experimental protocols and procedures to support the objectives of one or more laboratory research projects in a specific area of research. Assists in general laboratory activities, including maintenance of reagents and stocking of supplies. Files and monitors documents. The Research Associate I will be able to perform routine cellular, micro- and molecular biology procedures including, but not limited to Western, Northern blot, DNA/RNA, PCR, protein extraction, cell culture, Immunohistochemistry, and staining. This position does not have supervisory responsibilities. Primary Duties and Responsibilities: Performs a variety of routine laboratory tasks and procedures. Keeps accurate and detailed project records of experiments and results. May assist with animal husbandry. Maintains lab equipment and related records. Transports, processes, and logs samples. Maintains computer database with relevant clinical information. Performs lab maintenance duties, including glassware cleaning and sterilization. Maintains stocks of general lab materials, and places orders for lab equipment and supplies. Assists new students and fellows at the laboratory.
